\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4,\,k\not\in\{i,j\}}\{\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(j-k)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{kj}})+\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(k-i)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{j,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ik}})\}.

Proof.

The statement (i) follows immediately from
Proposition
3.1
(i) and (
3.7
).
By definition, for
1\leq i,j\leq 4
and
f\in H(\widehat{\sigma})_{K}
,
we have

(3.8)

\displaystyle 2(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{i,j})f)(1_{4})=\begin{cases}2\nu_{1}+\kappa_{1}+2&\text{if $i=j=1$},\\
2\nu_{2}-1&\text{if $i=j=3$},\\
0&\text{if $i<j$}.\end{cases}

Let
\lambda=(\kappa_{1},\delta_{2}-\delta_{3},\delta_{3})
and

\delta\in\{0,1\}
such that
\delta_{2}-\delta_{3}\geq\delta
.
We have

\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K}(\mathcal{R}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)},H(\sigma)_{K})=\mathbb{C}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}

by Lemma
2.6
and (
3.6
).
We define a
K
-homomorphism

\mathrm{P}_{\sigma}\colon\mathfrak{p}_{\mathbb{C}}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}H(\sigma)_{K}\to H(\sigma)_{K}
by
X\otimes f\mapsto\Pi_{\sigma}(X)f
.
Then there is a constant
c_{\delta}
such that

(3.9)

\displaystyle c_{\delta}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}=\mathrm{P}_{\sigma}\circ(\mathop{\mathrm{id}}\nolimits_{\mathfrak{p}_{\mathbb{C}}}\otimes(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}))\circ(\mathrm{I}^{\mathfrak{p}}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes\mathop{\mathrm{id}}\nolimits_{V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)}}),

since the right hand side is an element of

\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K}(\mathcal{R}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)},H(\sigma)_{K})
.

Let us consider the case of
\delta=0
.
Considering the image of
\xi_{i}\otimes u_{l}
under the both sides of (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle c_{0}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{i}})=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}})

\displaystyle(l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)},\ 1\leq i\leq 4).

Hence, in order to prove the statement (ii),
it suffices to show
c_{0}=2\nu_{1}
.
Using Lemmas
2.3
,
2.4
and
(
3.8
), for
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)}
,
we have

\displaystyle c_{0}\iota_{\sigma}({\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(c_{0}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}\!\left(\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}})\right)(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,1})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))\bigr)(1_{4})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{4}\bigl\{2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,k})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l+e_{k}}))(1_{4})\bigr\}

\displaystyle=2\nu_{1}\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}})).

Our task is to show the existence of
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)}

such that
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))\neq 0
.
When
\delta_{2}=\delta_{3}
, since

\displaystyle u_{\kappa_{1}e_{1}}+\sqrt{-1}u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{2}}=\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}(\zeta_{2}(\xi_{1})^{\kappa_{1}-1})=2^{-\kappa_{1}+1}\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}(\zeta_{2}(\zeta_{2}+\zeta_{1})^{\kappa_{1}-1}),

we have
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{\kappa_{1}e_{1}}))+\sqrt{-1}\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{2}}))=2^{-\kappa_{1}+1}
.
When
(\delta_{2},\delta_{3})=(1,0)
, since

\displaystyle u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}((\xi_{1})^{\kappa_{1}-1}\xi_{13})=2^{-\kappa_{1}-1}\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}((\zeta_{2}+\zeta_{1})^{\kappa_{1}-1}(\zeta_{23}+\zeta_{24}+\zeta_{13}+\zeta_{14})),

we have
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}}))=2^{-\kappa_{1}+1}
.
Therefore, we obtain the statement (ii).

Assume
(\delta_{2},\delta_{3})=(1,0)
.
Let us consider the case of
\delta=1
.
Considering the image of
\xi_{ij}\otimes u_{l}

under the both sides of (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle c_{1}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})=\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,i}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{j,j}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4,\,k\not\in\{i,j\}}\{\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(j-k)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{kj}})+\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(k-i)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{j,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ik}})\}

for
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,1,0)}
and

1\leq i<j\leq 4
.
Hence, in order to prove the statement (iii),
it suffices to show
c_{1}=2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})
.
Using Lemmas
2.3
,
2.4
and
(
3.8
), we have

\displaystyle c_{1}\,\iota_{\sigma}({\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}}))

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(c_{1}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}\!\bigl(\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,1}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{3,3}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{23}})+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{12}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{14}})-\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,4}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{34}})\bigr)\!(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,1}+E_{3,3})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}})\bigr)(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,2})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{23}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{23}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{2,3})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{12}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{12}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{3,4})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{14}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{14}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}-2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,4})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{34}}))\bigr)(1_{4})+\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{1,4}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{34}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}})).

Since
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-1)e_{1}+e_{13}}))=2^{-\kappa_{1}+1}\neq 0
, we obtain the statement (iii).
∎

3.4.
P_{(2,2)}
-principal series representations

Let
\sigma=D_{(\nu_{1},\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes D_{(\nu_{2},\kappa_{2})}

with
\nu_{1},\nu_{2}\in\mathbb{C}
and

\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 2}
such that
\kappa_{1}\geq\kappa_{2}
.
For
i\in\{1,2\}
, we set

\widehat{\sigma}_{i}=\chi_{(\nu_{i}+(\kappa_{i}-1)/2,\delta_{i})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{i}-(\kappa_{i}-1)/2,0)}

with
\delta_{i}\in\{0,1\}
such that

\delta_{i}\equiv\kappa_{i}\bmod 2
.
The group
K\cap M_{(2,2)}
is generated by the elements

\displaystyle\mathrm{k}_{\theta_{1},\theta_{2}}^{(2,2)}\qquad(\theta_{1},\theta_{2}\in\mathbb{R}),

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{diag}}\nolimits(\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\varepsilon_{3},\varepsilon_{4})\qquad(\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\varepsilon_{3},\varepsilon_{4}\in\{\pm 1\}).

Because of (
3.1
),
these elements act on
U_{\sigma,K\cap M_{(2,2)}}=\mathfrak{H}_{(\nu_{1},\kappa_{1}),K_{2}}\boxtimes_{\mathbb{C}}\mathfrak{H}_{(\nu_{2},\kappa_{2}),K_{2}}
by

\displaystyle\sigma(\mathrm{k}_{\theta_{1},\theta_{2}}^{(2,2)}){\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},q_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},q_{2})}=e^{\sqrt{-1}(q_{1}\theta_{1}+q_{2}\theta_{2})}{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},q_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},q_{2})},

\displaystyle\sigma(\mathop{\mathrm{diag}}\nolimits(\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\varepsilon_{3},\varepsilon_{4})){\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},q_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},q_{2})}=\varepsilon_{1}^{\kappa_{1}}\varepsilon_{3}^{\kappa_{2}}{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},\varepsilon_{1}\varepsilon_{2}q_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},\varepsilon_{3}\varepsilon_{4}q_{2})}

for
q_{1}\in\kappa_{1}+2\mathbb{Z}
and
q_{2}\in\kappa_{2}+2\mathbb{Z}

such that
|q_{1}|\geq\kappa_{1}
and
|q_{2}|\geq\kappa_{2}
.
By these equalities and Lemma
2.4
(ii),
for
\lambda\in\Lambda_{K}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K\cap M_{(2,2)}}(V_{\lambda},U_{\sigma,K\cap M_{(2,2)}})=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\mathbb{C}\,\eta_{\sigma}&\text{if $\lambda=(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)$},\\
\{0\}&\text{if $\lambda<_{\mathrm{lex}}(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)$},\end{cases}

where
\eta_{\sigma}\colon V_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}\to U_{\sigma,K\cap M_{(2,2)}}

is a
\mathbb{C}
-linear map defined by

\displaystyle\eta_{\sigma}(v_{l})=\begin{cases}{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},\kappa_{2})}&\text{if $l=(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{2}+\kappa_{2}e_{24}$},\\
{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},-\kappa_{2})}&\text{if $l=(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{2}+\kappa_{2}e_{23}$},\\
{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},-\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},\kappa_{2})}&\text{if $l=(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{1}+\kappa_{2}e_{14}$},\\
{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{1},-\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes{\mathrm{f}}_{(\widehat{\sigma}_{2},-\kappa_{2})}&\text{if $l=(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{1}+\kappa_{2}e_{13}$},\\
0&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

for
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}
.
By the Frobenius reciprocity law
[
16
, Theorem 1.14]
, we have

(3.10)

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K}(V_{\lambda},H(\sigma)_{K})=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\mathbb{C}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}&\text{if $\lambda=(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)$},\\
\{0\}&\text{if $\lambda<_{\mathrm{lex}}(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)$}\end{cases}

\displaystyle(\lambda\in\Lambda_{K}),

where

\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(v)(k)=\eta_{\sigma}(\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}(k)v)

for
v\in V_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}
and
k\in{K}
.
We call
\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}

the minimal
K
-type of
\Pi_{\sigma}
.

Let
\widehat{\sigma}=\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\boxtimes\widehat{\sigma}_{2}
.
We define a
\mathbb{C}
-linear form
\iota_{\sigma}\colon U_{\sigma}\to\mathbb{C}

by

\displaystyle\iota_{\sigma}(f_{1}\boxtimes f_{2})=f_{1}(1_{2})f_{2}(1_{2})

\displaystyle(f_{1}\in\mathfrak{H}_{(\nu_{1},\kappa_{1})},\ f_{2}\in\mathfrak{H}_{(\nu_{2},\kappa_{2})}).

Then
\Pi_{\sigma}
is embedded into a principal series representation

\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}
via

(3.11)

\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}\colon H(\sigma)\ni f\mapsto\iota_{\sigma}\circ f\in H(\widehat{\sigma}).

Proposition 3.3
.

Retain the notation.

(i) For
f\in H(\sigma)_{K}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi_{\sigma}(\mathcal{C}_{1})f=(2\nu_{1}+2\nu_{2})f,

\displaystyle\Pi_{\sigma}(\mathcal{C}_{2})f=\bigl(\nu_{1}^{2}+4\nu_{1}\nu_{2}+\nu_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{(\kappa_{1}-1)^{2}+(\kappa_{2}-1)^{2}}{4}\bigr)f,

\displaystyle\Pi_{\sigma}(\mathcal{C}_{3})f=\bigl(2\nu_{1}^{2}\nu_{2}+2\nu_{1}\nu_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{(\kappa_{2}-1)^{2}\nu_{1}+(\kappa_{1}-1)^{2}\nu_{2}}{2}\bigr)f,

\displaystyle\Pi_{\sigma}(\mathcal{C}_{4})f=\bigl(\nu_{1}^{2}-\tfrac{(\kappa_{1}-1)^{2}}{4}\bigr)\bigl(\nu_{2}^{2}-\tfrac{(\kappa_{2}-1)^{2}}{4}\bigr)f.

(ii) Assume
\kappa_{1}>\kappa_{2}
.
For
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2},0)}
and

1\leq i\leq 4
, we have

\displaystyle 2\nu_{1}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{i}})=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}}).

(iii)
For
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2}-1,0)}
and

1\leq i<j\leq 4
, we have

\displaystyle 2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})=\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,i}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{j,j}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4,\ k\not\in\{i,j\}}\{\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(j-k)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{kj}})+\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(k-i)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{j,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ik}})\}.

Proof.

The proof of this proposition is similar to that of
Proposition
3.2
.
The statement (i) follows immediately from
Proposition
3.1
(i) and (
3.11
).
By definition, for
1\leq i,j\leq 4
and
f\in H(\widehat{\sigma})_{K}
, we have

(3.12)

\displaystyle 2(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{i,j})f)(1_{4})=\begin{cases}2\nu_{1}+\kappa_{1}+2&\text{if $i=j=1$},\\
2\nu_{2}-\kappa_{2}-2&\text{if $i=j=4$},\\
0&\text{if $i<j$}.\end{cases}

Let
\lambda=(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)
and

\delta\in\{0,1\}
such that

\kappa_{1}-1\geq\kappa_{2}-\delta
.
We have

\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K}(\mathcal{R}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)},H(\sigma)_{K})=\mathbb{C}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}

by Lemma
2.6
and (
3.10
).
We define a
K
-homomorphism

\mathrm{P}_{\sigma}\colon\mathfrak{p}_{\mathbb{C}}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}H(\sigma)_{K}\to H(\sigma)_{K}
by
X\otimes f\mapsto\Pi_{\sigma}(X)f
.
Then there is a constant
c_{\delta}
such that

(3.13)

\displaystyle c_{\delta}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}=\mathrm{P}_{\sigma}\circ(\mathop{\mathrm{id}}\nolimits_{\mathfrak{p}_{\mathbb{C}}}\otimes(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}\circ\mathrm{B}_{\lambda}^{(1,\delta)}))\circ(\mathrm{I}^{\mathfrak{p}}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes\mathop{\mathrm{id}}\nolimits_{V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)}}),

since the right hand side is an element of

\mathop{\mathrm{Hom}}\nolimits_{K}(\mathcal{R}_{(1,\delta)}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}}V_{\lambda-(1,\delta,0)},H(\sigma)_{K})
.

Let us consider the case of
\delta=1
.
Considering the image of
\xi_{ij}\otimes u_{l}

under the both sides of (
3.13
), we have

\displaystyle c_{1}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})=\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,i}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{j,j}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69054pt+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4,\,k\not\in\{i,j\}}\{\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(j-k)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{kj}})+\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(k-i)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{j,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ik}})\}

for
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,1,0)}
and

1\leq i<j\leq 4
.
Hence, in order to prove the statement (iii),
it suffices to show
c_{1}=2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})
.
Let

l^{\prime}=(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2},0,0,0,0,l_{13}^{\prime},l_{14}^{\prime},l_{23}^{\prime},l_{24}^{\prime},0)\in S_{\lambda-(1,1,0)}.

Using Lemmas
2.3
,
2.4
and
(
3.11
),
we have

\displaystyle c_{1}\,\iota_{\sigma}({\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}}))

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(c_{1}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}\!\bigl(\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,1}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{4,4}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}})+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{24}})+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{13}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{34}})+\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{12}})\bigr)\!(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,1}+E_{4,4})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}}))\bigr)(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,2})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{24}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}(E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l^{\prime}+e_{24}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{3,4})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{13}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}(E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l^{\prime}+e_{13}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,3})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{34}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}(E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l^{\prime}+e_{34}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{2,4})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{12}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},0)}(E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l^{\prime}+e_{12}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}})).

Our task is to show the existence of
l^{\prime}

such that
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l^{\prime}+e_{14}}))\neq 0
if
\kappa_{2}\geq 2
.
Since

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=3}^{4}(\sqrt{-1})^{i+j}u_{(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{1}+(\kappa_{2}-1)e_{14}+e_{ij}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}(\xi_{1}^{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}\xi_{14}^{\kappa_{2}-1}\zeta_{24})

\displaystyle=2^{-\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}+2}(\sqrt{-1})^{-\kappa_{2}+1}\mathrm{q}_{\mathcal{R}}((\zeta_{2}+\zeta_{1})^{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}(\zeta_{24}-\zeta_{23}-\zeta_{13}+\zeta_{14})^{\kappa_{2}-1}\zeta_{24}),

we have

(3.14)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=3}^{4}(\sqrt{-1})^{i+j}\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{(\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2})e_{1}+(\kappa_{2}-1)e_{14}+e_{ij}}))=2^{-\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}+2}(\sqrt{-1})^{-\kappa_{2}+1}

and complete the proof of the statement (iii).

Assume
\kappa_{1}>\kappa_{2}
.
Let us consider the case of
\delta=0
.
Considering the image of
\xi_{i}\otimes u_{l}

under the both sides of (
3.13
), we have

\displaystyle c_{0}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{i}})=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}})

\displaystyle(l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)},\ 1\leq i\leq 4).

Hence, in order to prove the statement (ii),
it suffices to show
c_{0}=2\nu_{1}
.
Using Lemmas
2.3
,
2.4
and
(
3.12
), for
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)}
, we have

\displaystyle c_{0}\iota_{\sigma}({\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(c_{0}\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))(1_{4})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}\!\left(\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}})\right)(1_{4})

\displaystyle=2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,1})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))\bigr)(1_{4})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{4}\bigl\{2\bigl(\Pi_{\widehat{\sigma}}(E_{1,k})\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}}))\bigr)(1_{4})-\mathrm{I}_{\sigma}(\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(\tau_{\lambda}(E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{k}})u_{l+e_{k}}))(1_{4})\bigr\}

\displaystyle=2\nu_{1}\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}})).

By (
3.14
), we know that
there is
l\in S_{\lambda-(1,0,0)}

such that
\iota_{\sigma}(\eta_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{1}}))\neq 0
.
Therefore, we obtain the statement (ii).
∎

4.
Partial differential equations for Whittaker functions

4.1.
System of partial differential equations

In this subsection we give a system of partial differential equations
satisfied by radial parts of Whittaker functions using
Propositions
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3
.
Hereafter we say that we are in
case 1
,
case 2
and
case 3
if
\Pi_{\sigma}
are

P_{(1,1,1,1)}
,
P_{(2,1,1)}
and
P_{(2,2)}
-principal series representations, respectively.
We divide the cases 1 and 2 into subclasses according as
\delta_{i}
.

•

Case 1:

\sigma=\chi_{(\nu_{1},\delta_{1})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{2},\delta_{2})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{3},\delta_{3})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{4},\delta_{4})}
with
\delta_{1}\geq\delta_{2}\geq\delta_{3}\geq\delta_{4}
.

–

Case 1-(i):
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},\delta_{3},\delta_{4})=(0,0,0,0),(1,1,1,1).

–

Case 1-(ii):
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},\delta_{3},\delta_{4})=(1,0,0,0).

–

Case 1-(iii):
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},\delta_{3},\delta_{4})=(1,1,0,0).

–

Case 1-(iv):
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},\delta_{3},\delta_{4})=(1,1,1,0).

•

Case 2:

\sigma=D_{(\nu_{1},\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{2},\delta_{2})}\boxtimes\chi_{(\nu_{3},\delta_{3})}

with
\delta_{2}\geq\delta_{3}
.

–

Case 2-(i):
(\delta_{2},\delta_{3})=(0,0),(1,1).

–

Case 2-(ii):
(\delta_{2},\delta_{3})=(1,0).

•

Case 3:

\sigma=D_{(\nu_{1},\kappa_{1})}\boxtimes D_{(\nu_{2},\kappa_{2})}
with
\kappa_{1}\geq\kappa_{2}
.

We introduce the following notation to discuss three kinds of the generalized principal series representations simultaneously.

•

Case 1:

\kappa_{1}:=\delta_{1}-\delta_{4}
,
\kappa_{2}:=\delta_{2}-\delta_{3}
and

\nu_{1}^{\prime}:=\begin{cases}\nu_{1}&\text{case 1-(i),(ii),(iii)},\\
\nu_{4}&\text{case 1-(iv)}.\end{cases}

•

Case 2:

\delta_{1}:=\begin{cases}0&\text{if $\kappa_{1}$ is even},\\
1&\text{if $\kappa_{1}$ is odd},\end{cases}

\kappa_{2}:=\delta_{2}-\delta_{3}
and
\nu_{1}^{\prime}:=\nu_{1}
.

•

Case 3:

\delta_{1}:=\begin{cases}0&\text{if $\kappa_{1}$ is even},\\
1&\text{if $\kappa_{1}$ is odd},\end{cases}

\delta_{2}:=\begin{cases}0&\text{if $\kappa_{2}$ is even},\\
1&\text{if $\kappa_{2}$ is odd},\end{cases}

\delta_{3}:=0
and
\nu_{1}^{\prime}:=\nu_{1}.

For
1\leq i\leq 4
, we set

\displaystyle\gamma_{i}=\begin{cases}{\sigma}_{i}(\nu_{1},\nu_{2},\nu_{3},\nu_{4})&\text{case 1},\\
{\sigma}_{i}(\nu_{1}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2},\nu_{1}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2},\nu_{2},\nu_{3})&\text{case 2},\\
{\sigma}_{i}(\nu_{1}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2},\nu_{1}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2},\nu_{2}+\tfrac{\kappa_{2}-1}{2},\nu_{2}-\tfrac{\kappa_{2}-1}{2})&\text{case 3},\end{cases}

where

{\sigma}_{i}(a_{1},a_{2},a_{3},a_{4})=\sum_{1\leq k_{1}<k_{2}<\dots<k_{i}\leq 4}a_{k_{1}}a_{k_{2}}\cdots a_{k_{i}}

is the
i
-th elementary symmetric polynomial.
Then
\tau_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
is the minimal
K
-type of
\Pi_{\sigma}
,
and the results of Propositions
3.1
,
3.2
and
3.3

are summarized as follows:

•

For
f\in H(\sigma)_{K}
and
1\leq i\leq 4
, we have

(4.1)

\displaystyle\Pi_{\sigma}(\mathcal{C}_{i})f=\gamma_{i}f.

•

Assume
\kappa_{1}>\kappa_{2}
.
For
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
and

1\leq i\leq 4
, we have

(4.2)

\displaystyle 2\nu_{1}^{\prime}\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{i}})=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{k}}).

•

Assume
\kappa_{2}\geq 1
.
For
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2}-1,0)}
and

1\leq i<j\leq 4
, we have

(4.3)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&2(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})\,\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})=\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,i}^{\mathfrak{p}}+E_{j,j}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ij}})\\
&\hskip 5.69054pt+\sum_{1\leq k\leq 4,\,k\not\in\{i,j\}}\{\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(j-k)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{kj}})+\mathop{\mathrm{sgn}}\nolimits(k-i)\Pi_{\sigma}(E_{j,k}^{\mathfrak{p}})\hat{\eta}_{\sigma}(u_{l+e_{ik}})\}.\end{split}

Lemma 4.1

(
[
8
, Lemma 2.1]
)
.

Let
f
be a function in
C^{\infty}(N\backslash G;\psi_{1})
.
Then, for
1\leq i\leq j\leq 4
and
y={\rm diag}(y_{1}y_{2}y_{3}y_{4},y_{2}y_{3}y_{4},y_{3}y_{4},y_{4})\in A
, we have

\displaystyle(R(E_{i,j})f)(y)=\begin{cases}(-{\partial}_{i-1}+{\partial}_{i})f(y)&\mbox{ if }j=i,\\
2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{i}f(y)&\mbox{ if }j=i+1,\\
0&\mbox{ otherwise},\end{cases}

where
{\partial}_{0}=0
and
{\partial}_{i}=y_{i}\dfrac{{\partial}}{{\partial}y_{i}}

(1\leq i\leq 4)
.

Lemma 4.2
.

For
X,Y\in U(\mathfrak{g}_{\mathbb{C}})
, we write
X\equiv Y
if
X-Y\in(\mathbb{C}E_{1,3}+\mathbb{C}E_{1,4}+\mathbb{C}E_{2,4})U(\mathfrak{g}_{\mathbb{C}})
.
Then we have the following:

(4.4)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{1}&\equiv E_{1,1}+E_{2,2}+E_{3,3}+E_{4,4},\end{split}

(4.5)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{2}&\equiv\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq 4}(E_{i,i}+i-\tfrac{5}{2})(E_{j,j}+j-\tfrac{5}{2})-(E_{1,2})^{2}-(E_{2,3})^{2}-(E_{3,4})^{2}+E_{1,2}E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{2,3}E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{3,4}E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}},\end{split}

(4.6)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{3}&\equiv(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})+(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})\\
&\quad+(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})+(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})\\
&\quad-(E_{1,2})^{2}(E_{3,3}+E_{4,4}+2)+E_{1,2}(E_{3,3}+E_{4,4}+2)E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad-(E_{2,3})^{2}(E_{1,1}+E_{4,4})+E_{2,3}(E_{1,1}+E_{4,4})E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad-(E_{3,4})^{2}(E_{1,1}+E_{2,2}-2)+E_{3,4}(E_{1,1}+E_{2,2}-2)E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{k}},\end{split}

(4.7)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{4}&\equiv(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})\\
&\quad-(E_{1,2})^{2}(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})+E_{1,2}(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad-(E_{2,3})^{2}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})+E_{2,3}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad-(E_{3,4})^{2}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})+E_{3,4}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{1}{2})E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{2,3}E_{3,4}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{1,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}\\
&\quad+(E_{1,2})^{2}(E_{3,4})^{2}-(E_{1,2})^{2}E_{3,4}E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{1,2}(E_{3,4})^{2}E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{1,2}E_{3,4}E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}.\end{split}

Proof.

Recall that the Capelli elements
\mathcal{C}_{p}

(1\leq p\leq 4)
are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{p}=\sum_{\scriptstyle 1\leq i_{1}<i_{2}<\cdots<i_{p}\leq 4,\atop\scriptstyle w\in{\mathfrak{S}}_{p}}{\rm sgn}(w)\mathcal{E}_{i_{1},i_{w(1)}}\mathcal{E}_{i_{2},i_{w(2)}}\cdots\mathcal{E}_{i_{p},i_{w(p)}}.

In view of

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}(E_{i,i}+i-\tfrac{5}{2})=\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}E_{i,i},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq 4}\{(E_{i,i}+i-\tfrac{5}{2})(E_{j,j}+j-\tfrac{5}{2})-E_{i,j}E_{j,i}\}

and
E_{j,i}=E_{i,j}-E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}
, we get (
4.4
) and (
4.5
).

To consider
\mathcal{C}_{3}
and
\mathcal{C}_{4}
, we define subsets

{\mathfrak{S}}_{3,q}

(1\leq q\leq 3)
of
{\mathfrak{S}}_{3}
and

{\mathfrak{S}}_{4,q}

(1\leq q\leq 5)
of
{\mathfrak{S}}_{4}
by

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{S}}_{p,q}

\displaystyle=\{w\in{\mathfrak{S}}_{p}\mid\text{$w$ is a cyclic permutations of length $q$}\}

\displaystyle(1\leq q\leq p),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{S}_{4,5}

\displaystyle=\{(1\ 2)(3\ 4),(1\ 3)(2\ 4),(1\ 4)(2\ 3)\}.

We set

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{p,q}=\sum_{\scriptstyle 1\leq i_{1}<i_{2}<\cdots<i_{p}\leq 4,\atop\scriptstyle w\in{\mathfrak{S}}_{p,q}}{\rm sgn}(w)\mathcal{E}_{i_{1},i_{w(1)}}\mathcal{E}_{i_{2},i_{w(2)}}\cdots\mathcal{E}_{i_{p},i_{w(p)}}.

Then we can write
\mathcal{C}_{3}=\mathcal{C}_{3,1}+\mathcal{C}_{3,2}+\mathcal{C}_{3,3}
with

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{3,1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i<j<k\leq 4}(E_{i,i}+i-\tfrac{5}{2})(E_{j,j}+j-\tfrac{5}{2})(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{3,2}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\scriptstyle 1\leq i<j\leq 4,\,k\notin\{i,j\}}E_{i,j}E_{j,i}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{3,3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq i<j<k\leq 4}(E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{k,i}+E_{i,k}E_{j,i}E_{k,j}).

Using

\displaystyle E_{i,j}E_{j,i}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})

\displaystyle=E_{i,j}(E_{i,j}-E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}})(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})=(E_{i,j})^{2}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})-E_{i,j}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}

for
1\leq i<j\leq 4
and
k\notin\{i,j\}
, we know

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{3,2}

\displaystyle\equiv-\sum_{\scriptstyle 1\leq i\leq 3,\,k\notin\{i,i+1\}}\{(E_{i,i+1})^{2}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})-E_{i,i+1}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})E_{i,i+1}^{\mathfrak{k}}\}.

Similarly, for
1\leq i<j<k\leq 4
, we know

\displaystyle E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{k,i}

\displaystyle=E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{i,k}-E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{k}}\equiv-E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{i,k}^{\mathfrak{k}},

\displaystyle E_{i,k}E_{j,i}E_{k,j}\equiv 0.

Here we used
E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}E_{j,k}=E_{j,k}E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{i,k}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{3,3}

\displaystyle\equiv-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}.

Next we treat
\mathcal{C}_{4}
.
We can write
\mathcal{C}_{4}=\mathcal{C}_{4,1}+\mathcal{C}_{4,2}+\mathcal{C}_{4,3}+\mathcal{C}_{4,4}+\mathcal{C}_{4,5}
with

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,1}

\displaystyle=(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,2}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq 4}E_{i,j}E_{j,i}\sum_{k,l\notin\{i,j\},\,k<l}(E_{k,k}+k-\tfrac{5}{2})(E_{l,l}+l-\tfrac{5}{2}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\scriptstyle 1\leq i<j<k\leq 4,\,\scriptstyle l\notin\{i,j,k\}}(E_{i,j}E_{j,k}E_{k,i}+E_{i,k}E_{j,i}E_{k,j})(E_{l,l}+l-\tfrac{5}{2}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,4}

\displaystyle=-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{4,1}-E_{1,2}E_{2,4}E_{3,1}E_{4,3}-E_{1,3}E_{2,4}E_{3,2}E_{4,1}

\displaystyle\phantom{=\ }-E_{1,3}E_{2,1}E_{3,4}E_{4,2}-E_{1,4}E_{2,3}E_{3,1}E_{4,2}-E_{1,4}E_{2,1}E_{3,2}E_{4,3},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,5}

\displaystyle=E_{1,2}E_{2,1}E_{3,4}E_{4,3}+E_{1,3}E_{3,1}E_{2,4}E_{4,2}+E_{1,4}E_{4,1}E_{2,3}E_{3,2}.

In the same way as
\mathcal{C}_{3,2}
and
\mathcal{C}_{3,3}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,2}

\displaystyle\equiv-(E_{1,2})^{2}(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})+E_{1,2}(E_{3,3}+\tfrac{1}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=\ }-(E_{2,3})^{2}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})+E_{2,3}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})E_{2,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=\ }-(E_{3,4})^{2}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})+E_{3,4}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})(E_{2,2}-\tfrac{1}{2})E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,3}

\displaystyle\equiv-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}(E_{4,4}+\tfrac{3}{2})E_{1,3}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{2,3}E_{3,4}(E_{1,1}-\tfrac{3}{2})E_{2,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}.

Since

E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{4,1}\equiv-E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{1,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}

and

\displaystyle E_{1,2}E_{2,4}E_{3,1}E_{4,3}\equiv E_{1,3}E_{2,4}E_{3,2}E_{4,1}\equiv E_{1,3}E_{2,1}E_{3,4}E_{4,2}\equiv E_{1,4}E_{2,3}E_{3,1}E_{4,2}\equiv E_{1,4}E_{2,1}E_{3,2}E_{4,3}\equiv 0,

we find

\mathcal{C}_{4,4}\equiv E_{1,2}E_{2,3}E_{3,4}E_{1,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}.

As for
\mathcal{C}_{4,5}
, we have

\displaystyle E_{i,j}E_{j,i}E_{k,l}E_{l,k}

\displaystyle=E_{i,j}(E_{i,j}-E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}})E_{k,l}(E_{k,l}-E_{k,l}^{\mathfrak{k}})

\displaystyle=(E_{i,j})^{2}(E_{k,l})^{2}-(E_{i,j})^{2}E_{k,l}E_{k,l}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{i,j}(E_{k,l})^{2}E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{i,j}E_{k,l}E_{i,j}^{\mathfrak{k}}E_{k,l}^{\mathfrak{k}}

for
\{1,2,3,4\}=\{i,j,k,l\}
with
i<j
and
k<l
,
to get

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{4,5}\equiv(E_{1,2})^{2}(E_{3,4})^{2}-(E_{1,2})^{2}E_{3,4}E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}-E_{1,2}(E_{3,4})^{2}E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}+E_{1,2}E_{3,4}E_{1,2}^{\mathfrak{k}}E_{3,4}^{\mathfrak{k}}.

Thus we are done.
∎

Lemma 4.3
.

Retain the notation in Lemma
4.1
and
let
\varphi:V_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}\to{\rm Wh}(\Pi_{\sigma},\psi_{1})
be a
K
-homomorphism.

(i)
For
l=(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3},l_{4},l_{12},l_{13},l_{14},l_{23},l_{24},l_{34})\in S_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
, we have

(4.8)

\displaystyle({\partial}_{4}-\gamma_{1}){\varphi}(u_{l})(y)=0.

(ii)
Assume
\kappa_{1}>\kappa_{2}
.
For
l=(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3},l_{4},l_{12},l_{13},l_{14},l_{23},l_{24},l_{34})\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
, we have

\begin{split}&({\partial}_{1}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}}{2}-1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}})(y)=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{2}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}}{2}+l_{1}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}})(y)\\
&-l_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{2}+2e_{1}})(y)+l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}-e_{13}+e_{23}})(y)+l_{14}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}-e_{14}+e_{24}})(y)\\
&-l_{23}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}-e_{23}+e_{13}})(y)-l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}-e_{24}+e_{14}})(y)=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{3}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}}{2}-l_{4}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}})(y)\\
&+l_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{3}+2e_{4}})(y)+l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{13}+e_{14}})(y)-l_{14}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{14}+e_{13}})(y)\\
&+l_{23}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{23}+e_{24}})(y)-l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{24}+e_{23}})(y)=0,\end{split}

(-{\partial}_{3}+{\partial}_{4}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}}{2}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}})(y)=0.

(iii)
Assume
\kappa_{2}\geq 1
.
For
l=(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3},l_{4},l_{12},l_{13},l_{14},l_{23},l_{24},l_{34})\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2}-1,0)}
, we have

({\partial}_{2}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}+\kappa_{2}}{2}-1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{12}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{13}})(y)=0,

\begin{split}&({\partial}_{1}-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{3}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}{2}-l_{13}-l_{14}-l_{23}-l_{24}-1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{13}})(y)\\
&+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{23}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{12}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{14}})(y)\\
&+l_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{2}+e_{3}+e_{12}})(y)-l_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{3}+e_{2}+e_{12}})(y)-l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{13}+2e_{12}})(y)+l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{24}+e_{12}+e_{34}})(y)=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&({\partial}_{1}-{\partial}_{3}+{\partial}_{4}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}{2}+l_{4}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{14}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{24}})(y)\\
&+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{13}})(y)+l_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{2}+e_{4}+e_{12}})(y)+l_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}+e_{13}})(y)=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{3}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}{2}-l_{4}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{23}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{13}})(y)\\
&+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{24}})(y)-l_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{2}+e_{4}+e_{34}})(y)+l_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}+e_{24}})(y)=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{2}-{\partial}_{3}+{\partial}_{4}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-\kappa_{2}}{2}+l_{13}+l_{14}+l_{23}+l_{24}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{24}})(y)\\
&+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{14}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{34}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{23}})(y)\\
&+l_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{2}+e_{3}+e_{34}})(y)-l_{3}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{3}+e_{2}+e_{34}})(y)-l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{13}+e_{12}+e_{34}})(y)+l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{24}+2e_{34}})(y)=0,\end{split}

(-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{4}-\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}+\kappa_{2}}{2}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{34}})(y)+2\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{2}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{24}})(y)=0.

Proof.

The equation (
4.8
) is immediate from (
4.1
) with
i=1
, (
4.4
) and
Lemma
4.1
.
Let us show the first equation in (ii).
By (
4.2
)
and

E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{p}}=2E_{1,k}-E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{k}}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{4}(R(2E_{1,k}-E_{1,k}^{\mathfrak{k}}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{k}}))(y)-2\nu_{1}^{\prime}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)=0

for
l\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}.

Applying Lemmas
2.4
(i) and
4.1
, we find that

(4.9)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(2{\partial}_{1}-2\nu_{1}^{\prime}){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+4\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}})(y)\\
&-(l_{2}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+l_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{2}})(y)-l_{23}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{23}+e_{13}})(y)\\
&-l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{24}+e_{14}})(y)+l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{13}+e_{23}})(y)+l_{14}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{14}+e_{24}})(y)\\
&-(l_{3}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+l_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{3}})(y)+l_{23}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{23}+e_{12}})(y)\\
&-l_{34}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{34}+e_{14}})(y)-l_{12}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{12}+e_{23}})(y)+l_{14}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{14}+e_{34}})(y)\\
&-(l_{4}+1){\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+l_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{4}})(y)+l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{24}+e_{12}})(y)\\
&-l_{34}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{34}+e_{13}})(y)-l_{12}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{12}+e_{24}})(y)-l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{13}+e_{34}})(y)=0.\end{split}

By Lemma
2.3
(i), we know

\displaystyle l_{1}\{{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{2}})(y)+{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{3}})(y)+{\varphi}(u_{l-e_{1}+2e_{4}})(y)\}=-l_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y).

Similarly, Lemma
2.3
(ii) implies that

\displaystyle l_{23}\{-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{23}+e_{13}})(y)+{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{23}+e_{12}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{23}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y),

\displaystyle l_{24}\{-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{24}+e_{14}})(y)+{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{24}+e_{12}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{24}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y),

\displaystyle l_{12}\{-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{12}+e_{23}})(y)-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{12}+e_{24}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{12}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y),

\displaystyle l_{13}\{{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{13}+e_{23}})(y)-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{13}+e_{34}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{13}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y),

\displaystyle l_{14}\{{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}-e_{14}+e_{24}})(y)+{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{14}+e_{34}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{14}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y),

\displaystyle l_{34}\{-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{3}-e_{34}+e_{14}})(y)-{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{4}-e_{34}+e_{13}})(y)\}

\displaystyle=-l_{34}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y).

Then (
4.9
) can be written as

\displaystyle\{2{\partial}_{1}-2\nu_{1}^{\prime}-(l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}+l_{4}+3)-(l_{12}+l_{13}+l_{14}+l_{23}+l_{24}+l_{34})\}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{1}})(y)+4\pi\sqrt{-1}y_{1}{\varphi}(u_{l+e_{2}})(y)=0

as desired.
The other equations can be similarly confirmed.
∎

By (
4.8
),
we can define a function
\hat{\varphi}_{l}
on

by

(4.10)

\displaystyle{\varphi}(u_{l})(y)=(\sqrt{-1})^{-l_{1}+l_{3}-l_{13}+l_{24}}(-1)^{l_{2}+l_{14}+l_{23}}y_{1}^{3/2}y_{2}^{2}y_{3}^{3/2-\kappa_{2}}y_{4}^{\gamma_{1}}\hat{\varphi}_{l}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3})

for

.
We understand
\hat{\varphi}_{l}=0
if
l\notin S_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
.
Here is a system of partial differential equations for
\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}
.

Proposition 4.4
.

Retain the notation.

(i)
For
l=(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3},l_{4},l_{12},l_{13},l_{14},l_{23},l_{24},l_{34})\in S_{(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
, we have

(4.11)

\begin{split}&\{\Delta_{2}-(2\pi y_{1})\mathfrak{K}_{12}-(2\pi y_{2})\mathfrak{K}_{23}-(2\pi y_{3})\mathfrak{K}_{34}\}\hat{\varphi}_{l}=0,\end{split}

(4.12)

\begin{split}&\{\Delta_{3}+(2\pi y_{1})({\partial}_{2}-\gamma_{1})\mathfrak{K}_{12}+(2\pi y_{2})(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{3}-\gamma_{1}-\kappa_{2})\mathfrak{K}_{23}\\
&-(2\pi y_{3}){\partial}_{2}\mathfrak{K}_{34}+(2\pi y_{1})(2\pi y_{2})\mathfrak{K}_{13}+(2\pi y_{2})(2\pi y_{3})\mathfrak{K}_{24}\}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}=0\end{split}

and

(4.13)

\begin{split}&[\Delta_{4}-(2\pi y_{1})\{(-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{3}-\kappa_{2})(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})+(2\pi y_{3})^{2}\}\mathfrak{K}_{12}\\
&-(2\pi y_{2}){\partial}_{1}(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})\mathfrak{K}_{23}-(2\pi y_{3})\{{\partial}_{1}(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{2})+(2\pi y_{1})^{2}\}\mathfrak{K}_{34}\\
&+(2\pi y_{1})(2\pi y_{2})(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})\mathfrak{K}_{13}+(2\pi y_{2})(2\pi y_{3}){\partial}_{1}\mathfrak{K}_{24}\\
&+(2\pi y_{1})(2\pi y_{2})(2\pi y_{3})\mathfrak{K}_{14}+(2\pi y_{1})(2\pi y_{3})\mathfrak{K}_{12,34}]\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}=0,\end{split}

where
\Delta_{2}
,
\Delta_{3}
and
\Delta_{4}
are the differential operators defined by

\begin{split}\Delta_{2}&=-{\partial}_{1}^{2}-{\partial}_{2}^{2}-({\partial}_{3}-\kappa_{2})^{2}+{\partial}_{1}{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{2}({\partial}_{3}-\kappa_{2})+\gamma_{1}({\partial}_{3}-\kappa_{2})+(2\pi y_{1})^{2}+(2\pi y_{2})^{2}+(2\pi y_{3})^{2}-\gamma_{2},\end{split}

\begin{split}\Delta_{3}&={\partial}_{2}({\partial}_{1}-{\partial}_{3}+\kappa_{2})({\partial}_{1}-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{3}-\kappa_{2})-\gamma_{1}({\partial}_{1}^{2}+{\partial}_{2}^{2}-{\partial}_{1}{\partial}_{2}-{\partial}_{2}{\partial}_{3}+\kappa_{2}{\partial}_{2})\\
&\phantom{=}+(2\pi y_{1})^{2}(-{\partial}_{2}+\gamma_{1})+(2\pi y_{2})^{2}({\partial}_{1}-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})+(2\pi y_{3})^{2}{\partial}_{2}-\gamma_{3}\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\Delta_{4}&={\partial}_{1}({\partial}_{2}-{\partial}_{1})({\partial}_{3}-{\partial}_{2}-\kappa_{2})(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})+(2\pi y_{1})^{2}({\partial}_{3}-{\partial}_{2}-\kappa_{2})(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})\\
&\phantom{=}+(2\pi y_{2})^{2}{\partial}_{1}(-{\partial}_{3}+\gamma_{1}+\kappa_{2})+(2\pi y_{3})^{2}{\partial}_{1}({\partial}_{2}-{\partial}_{1})+(2\pi y_{1})^{2}(2\pi y_{3})^{2}-\gamma_{4},\end{split}

respectively.
Here we set

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{12}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{1}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{2}}+l_{2}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{1}}+l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}+e_{23}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}+e_{24}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{23}+e_{13}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{24}+e_{14}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{23}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{2}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{3}}+l_{3}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{2}}+l_{12}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{12}+e_{13}}+l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}+e_{12}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{24}+e_{34}}+l_{34}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{34}+e_{24}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{34}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{3}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}}+l_{4}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{3}}+l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}+e_{14}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}+e_{13}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{23}+e_{24}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{24}+e_{23}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{13}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{1}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{3}}+l_{3}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{1}}-l_{12}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{12}+e_{23}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}+e_{34}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{23}+e_{12}}-l_{34}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{34}+e_{14}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{24}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{2}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{4}}-l_{4}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{2}}+l_{12}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{12}+e_{14}}-l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}+e_{12}}-l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{23}+e_{34}}+l_{34}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{34}+e_{23}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{14}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=-l_{1}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{4}}-l_{4}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{1}}+l_{12}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{12}+e_{24}}+l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}+e_{34}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{24}+e_{12}}+l_{34}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{34}+e_{13}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{12,34}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l}

\displaystyle=l_{1}l_{3}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}-e_{3}+e_{2}+e_{4}}+l_{1}l_{4}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}-e_{4}+e_{2}+e_{3}}+l_{2}l_{3}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}-e_{3}+e_{1}+e_{4}}+l_{2}l_{4}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}-e_{4}+e_{1}+e_{3}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{1}(l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{2}-e_{13}+e_{14}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{2}-e_{14}+e_{13}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{2}-e_{23}+e_{24}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{1}+e_{2}-e_{24}+e_{23}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{2}(l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{1}-e_{13}+e_{14}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{23}-e_{14}+e_{13}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{1}-e_{23}+e_{24}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{2}+e_{1}-e_{24}+e_{23}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{3}(l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}-e_{13}+e_{23}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}-e_{14}+e_{24}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}-e_{23}+e_{13}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{3}+e_{4}-e_{24}+e_{14}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{4}(l_{13}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{3}-e_{13}+e_{23}}+l_{14}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{3}-e_{14}+e_{24}}+l_{23}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{3}-e_{23}+e_{13}}+l_{24}\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{4}+e_{3}-e_{24}+e_{14}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{13}(l_{14}+l_{23}+1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}+e_{24}}+l_{24}(l_{14}+l_{23}+1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{24}+e_{13}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{14}(l_{13}+l_{24}+1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}+e_{23}}+l_{23}(l_{13}+l_{24}+1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{23}+e_{14}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{13}l_{24}(\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}-e_{24}+2e_{14}}+\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{13}-e_{24}+2e_{23}})+l_{14}l_{23}(\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}-e_{23}+2e_{13}}+\hat{\varphi}_{l-e_{14}-e_{23}+2e_{24}})

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{13}(l_{13}-1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-2e_{13}+e_{14}+e_{23}}+l_{24}(l_{24}-1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-2e_{24}+e_{14}+e_{23}}

\displaystyle\phantom{=}+l_{14}(l_{14}-1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-2e_{14}+e_{13}+e_{24}}+l_{23}(l_{23}-1)\hat{\varphi}_{l-2e_{23}+e_{13}+e_{24}}.

(ii)
Assume
\kappa_{1}>\kappa_{2}
.
For
l=(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3},l_{4},l_{12},l_{13},l_{14},l_{23},l_{24},l_{34})\in S_{(\kappa_{1}-1,\kappa_{2},\delta_{3})}
, we have

(4.14)

\begin{split}&({\partial}_{1}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}}+(2\pi y_{1})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{2}}=0,\end{split}

(4.15)

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{1}+{\partial}_{2}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}-\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2}+l_{1})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{2}}-(2\pi y_{1})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}}+(2\pi y_{2})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{3}}+l_{2}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l-e_{2}+2e_{1}}\\
&+l_{13}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}-e_{13}+e_{23}}+l_{14}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}-e_{14}+e_{24}}+l_{23}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}-e_{23}+e_{13}}+l_{24}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{1}-e_{24}+e_{14}}=0,\end{split}

(4.16)

\begin{split}&(-{\partial}_{2}+{\partial}_{3}-\nu_{1}^{\prime}+\tfrac{\kappa_{1}-1}{2}-\kappa_{2}-l_{4})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{3}}-(2\pi y_{2})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{2}}+(2\pi y_{3})\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{4}}-l_{3}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l-e_{3}+2e_{4}}\\
&-l_{13}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{4}-e_{13}+e_{14}}-l_{14}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{4}-e_{14}+e_{13}}-l_{23}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{4}-e_{23}+e_{24}}-l_{24}\hat{{\varphi}}_{l+e_{4}-e_{24}+e_{23}}=0,\end{split}

(4.17)

\kappa_{2}\geq 1
.

4.1

.
